Model before rewriting:

find x: int(2..4)
find y: int(2..4)
find z: int(2..4)

such that

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#int_offset_1: bool
  find z#int_offset_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#int_offset_2),and([!(z#int_offset_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [z#int_offset_1,z#int_offset_2;int(1..)] [2, 4]) >= 2,SATInt(Offset, [z#int_offset_1,z#int_offset_2;int(1..)] [2, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
